A 'swing-out' refers to the trailer swinging left and right like a pendulum when, for some abnormal reason, only the trailer's wheels are braked. 'Hydroplaning' refers to a state in which, when driving at high speed on a wet road surface, the tires do not contact the surface, causing a loss of or inability to steer.
